Internet-Draft                                    Your Organization Here
Intended status: Standards Track                               I. Farrer
Expires: 27 May 2027                                 Deutsche Telekom AG
                                                        23 November 2026


                          DHCP Options in YANG
                 draft-lear-yangifying-dhcp-opts-latest

Abstract

   This memo specifies the process for creating YANG versions of DHCP
   server options.  By providing the appropriate data model, automation
   of both YANG-based management systems and DHCP systems can be
   improved.

About This Document

   This note is to be removed before publishing as an RFC.

   The latest revision of this draft can be found at
   https://elear.github.io/draft-lear-yangifying-dhcp-opts/draft-lear-
   yangifying-dhcp-opts.html.  Status information for this document may
   be found at https://datatracker.ietf.org/doc/draft-lear-yangifying-
   dhcp-opts/.

   Source for this draft and an issue tracker can be found at
   https://github.com/elear/draft-lear-yangifying-dhcp-opts.

1.  Introduction

   DHCP options contain whole lot of Cool Stuff that clients might like
   to know about their current environment.  The hint is in the name as
   to how this information has been transported: DHCP.  But those
   options can be used in other ways, either to configure clients
   through other substrates or even as part of a broader management
   framework to configure DHCP servers themselves.

   For example, if the inforamtion is properly structured it could be
   included at the EAP layer as part of TEAP /* Reference here */

   [RFC9243] defines the base YANG models for DHCPv6 servers, relays and
   clients, and includes YANG schema definitions for configuring the
   DHCPv6 options that are defined in [RFC9915].  [RFC9243], Appendix B
   describes rules and a general approach for extending the base YANG
   models with configuration nodes for additional DHCPv6 options.  This
   memo follows these rule and approach, and remains focused on DHCPv6.

   DHCP has been around a very long time, since the 1990s, and its
   predecessor BOOTP may have co-existed with dinosaurs.  The
   implication of this is that options specifications were strictly
   narrative alongside a packet diagram.  The specification of the
   option contents themselves was not governed by a former data model.
   This draft changes how new options get defined so that YANG is now
   used to defined the option contents.  For this reason it updates /*
   reference here */.

   Finally, we provide an initial registry of server options that have
   already been defined.  For the sake of sanity, we have not attempted
   to go beyond simple typing found in /* reference here */.

   i# DHCPv6 Option Models

1.1.  RFC 3319: DHCPv6 options model ietf-dhcpv6-opt-sip

   This model covers the DHCPv6 options specified in [RFC3319]: Dynamic
   Host Configuration Protocol (DHCPv6) Options for Session Initiation
   Protocol (SIP) Servers.

                +==================+=====================+
                | IANA option code | IANA option name    |
                +==================+=====================+
                |        21        | OPTION_SIP_SERVER_D |
                +------------------+---------------------+
                |        22        | OPTION_SIP_SERVER_A |
                +------------------+---------------------+

                                 Table 1

   module: ietf-dhcpv6-opt-sip
     augment /dhc6-srv:dhcpv6-server/dhc6-srv:option-sets/
            dhc6-srv:option-set:
       +--rw sip-server-domain-name-list-option!
          +--rw sip-server-domain-name* inet:domain-name
       +--rw sip-server-address-list-option!
          +--rw sip-server-address* inet:ipv6-address

   <CODE BEGINS> file "ietf-dhcpv6-opt-sip.yang"
   module ietf-dhcpv6-opt-sip {
     yang-version 1.1;
     namespace "urn:ietf:params:xml:ns:yang:"
       + "ietf-dhcpv6-opt-sip";
     prefix sip;

     import ietf-inet-types {
       prefix inet;
       reference
         "RFC 6991: Common YANG Data Types";
     }
     import ietf-dhcpv6-server {
       prefix dhc6-srv;
       reference
         "RFC 9243: A YANG Data Model for DHCPv6
          Configuration";
     }

     organization
       "IETF Dynamic Host Configuration (DHC) Working Group";
     contact
       "DHC Working Group
        WG Web: <https://datatracker.ietf.org/wg/dhc/>
        WG List: <mailto:dhcwg@ietf.org>";
     description
        "This module defines the DHCPv6 options from RFC 3319: Dynamic
         Host Configuration Protocol (DHCPv6) Options for Session
         Initiation Protocol (SIP) Servers.";
     reference "RFC 3319: Dynamic Host Configuration Protocol (DHCPv6)
       Options for Session Initiation Protocol (SIP) Servers";

     revision 2026-11-19 {
       description "Initial revision.";
       reference "RFC 3319: Dynamic Host Configuration Protocol (DHCPv6)
         Options for Session Initiation Protocol (SIP) Servers";
     }

     grouping sip-server-domain-name-list-option-group {
       description "OPTION_SIP_SERVER_D (21) SIP Servers Domain-Name
         List.";
       reference "RFC 3319: Dynamic Host Configuration Protocol (DHCPv6)
         Options for Session Initiation Protocol (SIP) Servers";
       container sip-server-domain-name-list-option {
         presence "Configures this DHCPv6 option.";
         description "OPTION_SIP_SERVER_D (21) SIP Servers Domain-Name
           List option.";
         leaf-list sip-server-domain-name {
           type inet:domain-name;
           description "SIP server domain name.";
         }
       }
     }

     grouping sip-server-address-list-option-group {
       description "OPTION_SIP_SERVER_A (22) SIP Servers IPv6 Address
         List.";
       reference "RFC 3319: Dynamic Host Configuration Protocol (DHCPv6)
         Options for Session Initiation Protocol (SIP) Servers";
       container sip-server-address-list-option {
         presence "Configures this DHCPv6 option.";
         description "OPTION_SIP_SERVER_A (22) SIP Servers IPv6 Address
           List option.";
         leaf-list sip-server-address {
           type inet:ipv6-address;
           description "SIP server IPv6 address.";
         }
       }
     }

     augment
       "/dhc6-srv:dhcpv6-server/"
         + "dhc6-srv:option-sets/"
         + "dhc6-srv:option-set" {
       description "Add RFC 3319 options.";
       uses sip-server-domain-name-list-option-group;
       uses sip-server-address-list-option-group;
     }

   }
   <CODE ENDS>

1.2.  RFC 3646: DHCPv6 options model ietf-dhcpv6-opt-dns

   This model covers the DHCPv6 options specified in [RFC3646]: DNS
   Configuration options for DHCPv6.

                 +==================+====================+
                 | IANA option code | IANA option name   |
                 +==================+====================+
                 |        23        | OPTION_DNS_SERVERS |
                 +------------------+--------------------+
                 |        24        | OPTION_DOMAIN_LIST |
                 +------------------+--------------------+

                                  Table 2

   module: ietf-dhcpv6-opt-dns
     augment /dhc6-srv:dhcpv6-server/dhc6-srv:option-sets/
            dhc6-srv:option-set:
       +--rw dns-servers-option!
          +--rw dns-server* inet:ipv6-address
       +--rw domain-list-option!
          +--rw domain-name* inet:domain-name

   <CODE BEGINS> file "ietf-dhcpv6-opt-dns.yang"
   module ietf-dhcpv6-opt-dns {
     yang-version 1.1;
     namespace "urn:ietf:params:xml:ns:yang:"
       + "ietf-dhcpv6-opt-dns";
     prefix dns;

     import ietf-inet-types {
       prefix inet;
       reference
         "RFC 6991: Common YANG Data Types";
     }
     import ietf-dhcpv6-server {
       prefix dhc6-srv;
       reference
         "RFC 9243: A YANG Data Model for DHCPv6
          Configuration";
     }

     organization
       "IETF Dynamic Host Configuration (DHC) Working Group";
     contact
       "DHC Working Group
        WG Web: <https://datatracker.ietf.org/wg/dhc/>
        WG List: <mailto:dhcwg@ietf.org>";
     description
        "This module defines the DHCPv6 options from RFC 3646: DNS
         Configuration options for DHCPv6.";
     reference "RFC 3646: DNS Configuration options for DHCPv6";

     revision 2026-11-19 {
       description "Initial revision.";
       reference "RFC 3646: DNS Configuration options for DHCPv6";
     }

     grouping dns-servers-option-group {
       description "OPTION_DNS_SERVERS (23) DNS Recursive Name Server
         Option.";
       reference "RFC 3646: DNS Configuration options for DHCPv6";
       container dns-servers-option {
         presence "Configures this DHCPv6 option.";
         description "OPTION_DNS_SERVERS (23) DNS Recursive Name Server
           Option option.";
         leaf-list dns-server {
           type inet:ipv6-address;
           description "DNS recursive name server address.";
         }
       }
     }

     grouping domain-list-option-group {
       description "OPTION_DOMAIN_LIST (24) Domain Search List Option.";
       reference "RFC 3646: DNS Configuration options for DHCPv6";
       container domain-list-option {
         presence "Configures this DHCPv6 option.";
         description "OPTION_DOMAIN_LIST (24) Domain Search List Option
           option.";
         leaf-list domain-name {
           type inet:domain-name;
           description "Domain search name.";
         }
       }
     }

     augment
       "/dhc6-srv:dhcpv6-server/"
         + "dhc6-srv:option-sets/"
         + "dhc6-srv:option-set" {
       description "Add RFC 3646 options.";
       uses dns-servers-option-group;
       uses domain-list-option-group;
     }

   }
   <CODE ENDS>

1.3.  RFC 3898: DHCPv6 options model ietf-dhcpv6-opt-nis

   This model covers the DHCPv6 options specified in [RFC3898]: Network
   Information Service (NIS) Configuration Options for DHCPv6.

              +==================+=========================+
              | IANA option code | IANA option name        |
              +==================+=========================+
              |        27        | OPTION_NIS_SERVERS      |
              +------------------+-------------------------+
              |        28        | OPTION_NISP_SERVERS     |
              +------------------+-------------------------+
              |        29        | OPTION_NIS_DOMAIN_NAME  |
              +------------------+-------------------------+
              |        30        | OPTION_NISP_DOMAIN_NAME |
              +------------------+-------------------------+

                                 Table 3

   module: ietf-dhcpv6-opt-nis
     augment /dhc6-srv:dhcpv6-server/dhc6-srv:option-sets/
            dhc6-srv:option-set:
       +--rw nis-servers-option!
          +--rw nis-server* inet:ipv6-address
       +--rw nisp-servers-option!
          +--rw nisp-server* inet:ipv6-address
       +--rw nis-domain-name-option!
          +--rw nis-domain-name? inet:domain-name
       +--rw nisp-domain-name-option!
          +--rw nisp-domain-name? inet:domain-name

   <CODE BEGINS> file "ietf-dhcpv6-opt-nis.yang"
   module ietf-dhcpv6-opt-nis {
     yang-version 1.1;
     namespace "urn:ietf:params:xml:ns:yang:"
       + "ietf-dhcpv6-opt-nis";
     prefix nis;

     import ietf-inet-types {
       prefix inet;
       reference
         "RFC 6991: Common YANG Data Types";
     }
     import ietf-dhcpv6-server {
       prefix dhc6-srv;
       reference
         "RFC 9243: A YANG Data Model for DHCPv6
          Configuration";
     }

     organization
       "IETF Dynamic Host Configuration (DHC) Working Group";
     contact
       "DHC Working Group
        WG Web: <https://datatracker.ietf.org/wg/dhc/>
        WG List: <mailto:dhcwg@ietf.org>";
     description
        "This module defines the DHCPv6 options from RFC 3898: Network
         Information Service (NIS) Configuration Options for DHCPv6.";
     reference "RFC 3898: Network Information Service (NIS) Configuration
       Options for DHCPv6";

     revision 2026-11-19 {
       description "Initial revision.";
       reference "RFC 3898: Network Information Service (NIS)
         Configuration Options for DHCPv6";
     }

     grouping nis-servers-option-group {
       description "OPTION_NIS_SERVERS (27) Network Information Service
         Servers Option.";
       reference "RFC 3898: Network Information Service (NIS)
         Configuration Options for DHCPv6";
       container nis-servers-option {
         presence "Configures this DHCPv6 option.";
         description "OPTION_NIS_SERVERS (27) Network Information Service
           Servers Option option.";
         leaf-list nis-server {
           type inet:ipv6-address;
           description "NIS server address.";
         }
       }
     }

     grouping nisp-servers-option-group {
       description "OPTION_NISP_SERVERS (28) Network Information Service+
         Servers Option.";
       reference "RFC 3898: Network Information Service (NIS)
         Configuration Options for DHCPv6";
       container nisp-servers-option {
         presence "Configures this DHCPv6 option.";
         description "OPTION_NISP_SERVERS (28) Network Information
           Service+ Servers Option option.";
         leaf-list nisp-server {
           type inet:ipv6-address;
           description "NIS+ server address.";
         }
       }
     }

     grouping nis-domain-name-option-group {
       description "OPTION_NIS_DOMAIN_NAME (29) Network Information
         Service Domain Name Option.";
       reference "RFC 3898: Network Information Service (NIS)
         Configuration Options for DHCPv6";
       container nis-domain-name-option {
         presence "Configures this DHCPv6 option.";
         description "OPTION_NIS_DOMAIN_NAME (29) Network Information
           Service Domain Name Option option.";
         leaf nis-domain-name {
           type inet:domain-name;
           description "NIS domain name.";
         }
       }
     }

     grouping nisp-domain-name-option-group {
       description "OPTION_NISP_DOMAIN_NAME (30) Network Information

     augment /dhc6-clnt:dhcpv6-client/dhc6-clnt:client-if/
            dhc6-clnt:client-configured-options:
       +--rw lq-query-option!
          +--rw query-type? uint8
          +--rw link-address? inet:ipv6-address
          +--rw query-address? inet:ipv6-address
          +--rw client-id? binary
       +--rw client-data-option!
          +--rw client-id? binary
          +--rw clt-time? uint64
          +--rw ia-data? binary
       +--rw clt-time-option!
          +--rw clt-time? uint64
       +--rw lq-relay-data-option!
          +--rw peer-address? inet:ipv6-address
          +--rw relay-message? binary
       +--rw lq-client-link-option!
          +--rw client-link-address* inet:ipv6-address

   <CODE BEGINS> file "ietf-dhcpv6-opt-leasequery.yang"
   module ietf-dhcpv6-opt-leasequery {
     yang-version 1.1;
     namespace "urn:ietf:params:xml:ns:yang:"
       + "ietf-dhcpv6-opt-leasequery";
     prefix leaseque;

     import ietf-inet-types {
       prefix inet;
       reference
         "RFC 6991: Common YANG Data Types";

       "IETF Dynamic Host Configuration (DHC) Working Group";

        WG Web: <https://datatracker.ietf.org/wg/dhc/>
        WG List: <mailto:dhcwg@ietf.org>";
     description
        "This module defines the DHCPv6 options from RFC 5007: DHCPv6
         Leasequery.";
     reference "RFC 5007: DHCPv6 Leasequery";

       description "Initial revision.";
       reference "RFC 5007: DHCPv6 Leasequery";
     }

     grouping lq-query-option-group {
       description "OPTION_LQ_QUERY (44) Leasequery Query Option.";
       reference "RFC 5007: DHCPv6 Leasequery";
       container lq-query-option {
         presence "Configures this DHCPv6 option.";

           option.";
         leaf query-type {
           type uint8;
           description "Leasequery query type.";
         }
         leaf link-address {
           type inet:ipv6-address;

           type inet:ipv6-address;

         }
         leaf client-id {
           type binary;
           description "Queried client DUID, when applicable.";
         }
       }
     }

     grouping client-data-option-group {
       description "OPTION_CLIENT_DATA (45) Client Data Option.";
       reference "RFC 5007: DHCPv6 Leasequery";

         presence "Configures this DHCPv6 option.";
         description "OPTION_CLIENT_DATA (45) Client Data Option option.";
         leaf client-id {
           type binary;
           description "Client DUID.";
         }
         leaf clt-time {
           type uint64;
           description "Client last-transaction time.";
         }
         leaf ia-data {
           type binary;
           description "Identity-association data.";
         }
       }
     }

     grouping clt-time-option-group {
       description "OPTION_CLT_TIME (46) Client Last-Transaction Time
         Option.";
       reference "RFC 5007: DHCPv6 Leasequery";
       container clt-time-option {
         presence "Configures this DHCPv6 option.";
         description "OPTION_CLT_TIME (46) Client Last-Transaction Time
           Option option.";

           type uint64;
           description "Client last-transaction time.";
         }
       }
     }

     grouping lq-relay-data-option-group {
       description "OPTION_LQ_RELAY_DATA (47) Leasequery Relay Data
         Option.";
       reference "RFC 5007: DHCPv6 Leasequery";
       container lq-relay-data-option {
         presence "Configures this DHCPv6 option.";
         description "OPTION_LQ_RELAY_DATA (47) Leasequery Relay Data

           type inet:ipv6-address;
           description "Peer address.";
         }

           type binary;

         }
       }
     }

     grouping lq-client-link-option-group {

         Option.";

       container lq-client-link-option {

           Option option.";
         leaf-list client-link-address {
           type inet:ipv6-address;
           description "Client link address.";
         }
       }
     }

     augment
       "/dhc6-clnt:dhcpv6-client/"
         + "dhc6-clnt:client-if/"
         + "dhc6-clnt:client-configured-options" {
       description "Add RFC 5007 options.";

       uses client-data-option-group;
       uses clt-time-option-group;

       uses lq-client-link-option-group;

         "RFC 6991: Common YANG Data Types";

        "This module defines the DHCPv6 options from RFC 5970: DHCPv6
         Options for Network Boot.";
     reference "RFC 5970: DHCPv6 Options for Network Boot";

     revision 2026-11-19 {
       description "Initial revision.";
       reference "RFC 5970: DHCPv6 Options for Network Boot";
     }

     grouping bootfile-url-option-group {
       description "OPT_BOOTFILE_URL (59) Boot File URL Option.";
       reference "RFC 5970: DHCPv6 Options for Network Boot";
       container bootfile-url-option {
         presence "Configures this DHCPv6 option.";
         description "OPT_BOOTFILE_URL (59) Boot File URL Option option.";
         leaf bootfile-url {
           type inet:uri;
           description "Boot file URL.";
         }
       }
     }

     grouping bootfile-param-option-group {
       description "OPT_BOOTFILE_PARAM (60) Boot File Parameters Option.";
       reference "RFC 5970: DHCPv6 Options for Network Boot";
       container bootfile-param-option {

         description "OPT_BOOTFILE_PARAM (60) Boot File Parameters Option
           option.";
         leaf-list bootfile-parameter {
           type binary;
           description "Boot file parameter data.";
         }
       }
     }

     grouping client-arch-type-option-group {
       description "OPTION_CLIENT_ARCH_TYPE (61) Client System
         Architecture Type Option.";
       reference "RFC 5970: DHCPv6 Options for Network Boot";
       container client-arch-type-option {
         presence "Configures this DHCPv6 option.";
         description "OPTION_CLIENT_ARCH_TYPE (61) Client System
           Architecture Type Option option.";
         leaf-list architecture-type {
           type uint16;
           description "Client architecture type.";
         }
       }
     }

     grouping nii-option-group {

       reference "RFC 5970: DHCPv6 Options for Network Boot";
       container nii-option {
         presence "Configures this DHCPv6 option.";
         description "OPTION_NII (62) Network Interface Identifier Option
           option.";
         leaf interface-type {
           type uint8;
           description "Network interface type.";
         }
         leaf major-version {
           type uint8;
           description "Major version.";
         }
         leaf minor-version {
           type uint8;
           description "Minor version.";
         }
       }
     }

     augment
       "/dhc6-srv:dhcpv6-server/"
         + "dhc6-srv:option-sets/"
         + "dhc6-srv:option-set" {
       description "Add RFC 5970 options.";
       uses bootfile-url-option-group;
       uses bootfile-param-option-group;
       uses client-arch-type-option-group;
       uses nii-option-group;
     }

   }

1.38.  RFC 7598: DHCPv6 options model ietf-dhcpv6-opt-s46

   This model covers the DHCPv6 options specified in [RFC7598]: Mapping
   of Address and Port with Encapsulation (MAP-E) and Translation (MAP-
   T).

               +==================+=======================+
               | IANA option code | IANA option name      |
               +==================+=======================+
               |        89        | OPTION_S46_RULE       |
               +------------------+-----------------------+
               |        90        | OPTION_S46_BR         |
               +------------------+-----------------------+
               |        91        | OPTION_S46_DMR        |
               +------------------+-----------------------+
               |        92        | OPTION_S46_V4V6BIND   |
               +------------------+-----------------------+
               |        93        | OPTION_S46_PORTPARAMS |
               +------------------+-----------------------+
               |        94        | OPTION_S46_CONT_MAPE  |
               +------------------+-----------------------+
               |        95        | OPTION_S46_CONT_MAPT  |
               +------------------+-----------------------+
               |        96        | OPTION_S46_CONT_LW    |
               +------------------+-----------------------+

                                 Table 38

   module: ietf-dhcpv6-opt-s46
     augment /dhc6-srv:dhcpv6-server/dhc6-srv:option-sets/
            dhc6-srv:option-set:
       +--rw s46-rule-option!
          +--rw rule-data? binary
       +--rw s46-br-option!
          +--rw border-relay-address* inet:ipv6-address
       +--rw s46-dmr-option!
          +--rw default-mapping-prefix? inet:ipv6-prefix
       +--rw s46-v4v6bind-option!
          +--rw binding-data? binary
       +--rw s46-portparams-option!
          +--rw offset? uint8
          +--rw psid-length? uint8
          +--rw psid? uint16
       +--rw s46-cont-mape-option!
          +--rw container-data* binary
       +--rw s46-cont-mapt-option!
          +--rw container-data* binary
       +--rw s46-cont-lw-option!
          +--rw container-data* binary

   <CODE BEGINS> file "ietf-dhcpv6-opt-s46.yang"

        "This module defines the DHCPv6 options from RFC 7598: Mapping of
         Address and Port with Encapsulation (MAP-E) and Translation

   Relay Agent Port Option.

                 +==================+===================+
                 |       135        | OPTION_RELAY_PORT |
                 +------------------+-------------------+

                                 Table 46

   module: ietf-dhcpv6-opt-relay-port
     augment /dhc6-rly:dhcpv6-relay/dhc6-rly:relay-if/
            dhc6-rly:relay-options:
       +--rw relay-port-option!
          +--rw relay-port? uint16

   <CODE BEGINS> file "ietf-dhcpv6-opt-relay-port.yang"

       "DHC Working Group
        WG Web: <https://datatracker.ietf.org/wg/dhc/>
        WG List: <mailto:dhcwg@ietf.org>";
     description
        "This module defines the DHCPv6 options from RFC 8357: DHCPv6
         Relay Agent Port Option.";
     reference "RFC 8357: DHCPv6 Relay Agent Port Option";

       description "Initial revision.";
       reference "RFC 8357: DHCPv6 Relay Agent Port Option";
     }

1.68.  RFC5007: ietf-dhcpv6-opt-leasequery YANG Model

   This model covers the DHCPv6 options specified in [RFC5007]: DHCPv6
   Leasequery.

               +==================+=======================+
               | IANA option code | IANA option name      |
               +==================+=======================+
               |        44        | OPTION_LQ_QUERY       |
               +------------------+-----------------------+
               |        45        | OPTION_CLIENT_DATA    |
               +------------------+-----------------------+
               |        46        | OPTION_CLT_TIME       |
               +------------------+-----------------------+
               |        47        | OPTION_LQ_RELAY_DATA  |
               +------------------+-----------------------+
               |        48        | OPTION_LQ_CLIENT_LINK |
               +------------------+-----------------------+

                                 Table 68

   module: ietf-dhcpv6-opt-leasequery
     augment /dhc6-clnt:dhcpv6-client/dhc6-clnt:client-if/
            dhc6-clnt:client-configured-options:
       +--rw lq-query-option!
          +--rw query-type? uint8
          +--rw link-address? inet:ipv6-address
          +--rw query-address? inet:ipv6-address
          +--rw client-id? binary
       +--rw client-data-option!
          +--rw client-id? binary
          +--rw clt-time? uint64
          +--rw ia-data? binary
       +--rw clt-time-option!
          +--rw clt-time? uint64
       +--rw lq-relay-data-option!
          +--rw peer-address? inet:ipv6-address
          +--rw relay-message? binary
       +--rw lq-client-link-option!
          +--rw client-link-address* inet:ipv6-address

   <CODE BEGINS> file "ietf-dhcpv6-opt-leasequery.yang"

          Configuration";

        WG List: <mailto:dhcwg@ietf.org>";

        "This module defines the DHCPv6 options from RFC 5007: DHCPv6
         Leasequery.";
     reference "RFC 5007: DHCPv6 Leasequery";

       description "Initial revision.";
       reference "RFC 5007: DHCPv6 Leasequery";
     }

           type uint8;
           description "Leasequery query type.";

           type inet:ipv6-address;

       }

       }
     }

         Option.";

           type uint64;
           description "Client last-transaction time.";
         }
       }
     }

     grouping lq-relay-data-option-group {

       container lq-relay-data-option {

           type inet:ipv6-address;

       }
     }

     grouping lq-client-link-option-group {

         }
       }
     }

                 +==================+===================+

                 +------------------+-------------------+
                 |        50        | OPTION_MIP6_VDINF |
                 +------------------+-------------------+

       +--rw mip6-haa-option!

          +--rw container-data* binary
       +--rw s46-cont-mapt-option!
          +--rw container-data* binary
       +--rw s46-cont-lw-option!

       +--rw f-dns-host-name-option!
          +--rw dns-host-name? inet:domain-name
       +--rw f-dns-zone-name-option!
          +--rw dns-zone-name? inet:domain-name
       +--rw f-dns-flags-option!
          +--rw dns-flags? uint8
       +--rw f-expiration-time-option!
          +--rw expiration-time? uint32
       +--rw f-max-unacked-bndupd-option!
          +--rw max-unacked-binding-updates? uint32
       +--rw f-mclt-option!
          +--rw mclt? uint32
       +--rw f-partner-lifetime-option!
          +--rw partner-lifetime? uint32
       +--rw f-partner-lifetime-sent-option!
          +--rw partner-lifetime-sent? uint32
       +--rw f-partner-down-time-option!
          +--rw partner-down-time? uint32
       +--rw f-partner-raw-clt-time-option!
          +--rw partner-raw-clt-time? uint32
       +--rw f-protocol-version-option!
          +--rw protocol-version? uint32
       +--rw f-keepalive-time-option!
          +--rw keepalive-time? uint32
       +--rw f-reconfigure-data-option!
          +--rw reconfigure-data? binary
       +--rw f-relationship-name-option!
          +--rw relationship-name? string
       +--rw f-server-flags-option!
          +--rw server-flags? uint8
       +--rw f-server-state-option!
          +--rw server-state? uint8
       +--rw f-start-time-of-state-option!
          +--rw start-time-of-state? uint32
       +--rw f-state-expiration-time-option!
          +--rw state-expiration-time? uint32

   Relay Agent Port Option.

                 +==================+===================+

            dhc6-rly:relay-options:
       +--rw relay-port-option!
          +--rw relay-port? uint16

         }

           type binary;
           description "Additional redirect data.";
         }
       }
     }

     augment
       "/dhc6-srv:dhcpv6-server/"
         + "dhc6-srv:option-sets/"
         + "dhc6-srv:option-set" {
       description "Add RFC 8572 options.";

     }

   }

1.113.  Conventions and Definitions

   The key words "MUST", "MUST NOT", "REQUIRED", "SHALL", "SHALL NOT",
   "SHOULD", "SHOULD NOT", "RECOMMENDED", "NOT RECOMMENDED", "MAY", and
   "OPTIONAL" in this document are to be interpreted as described in
   BCP 14 [RFC2119] [RFC8174] when, and only when, they appear in all
   capitals, as shown here.
